# Cloud benchmark results

- Date: 2026-10-21
- Source: 49f41f35 plus any uncommitted changes at upload time
- Database: Cloud SQL POSTGRES_17 (server 17.11), db-custom-4-16384, europe-west2-a, private IP, SSL
- Client: Compute Engine n2d-standard-4, same zone
- Runs per benchmark: 6

## Where the time goes

Medians of 6 runs. Each row splits the time the client measured into:

- **In database**: server time for the statements the operation sent, from `pg_stat_statements`.
- **Network and client**: the rest: round trips, transferring results, and driver and client work in Go.

For reference, a `SELECT 1` takes **336.1 µs** (2.4 µs of it in the database), the floor for one statement's network and client time.
Every operation below sends one statement unless the network column says otherwise.

**Spread** is the largest difference between one run's total and the median.
